It is about one hundred and fifty actions that will be carried out this year and that we certainly have to incorporate because they are necessary to improve the dignity in the provision of service and also the attention to the dignity of those who will receive assistance. With regard to training, we place great emphasis on the continuous and necessary formation of the State Security Forces and Bodies and also on the maintenance of the State's comprehensive emergency digital radio communications system, which is one of the essential tools for the daily work of the State Security Forces and Bodies. I would also like to tell you that these budgets allow us to continue with the commitment to purchase and purchase bulletproof vests for both sexes. A file for the acquisition of 5,200 vests for the National Police is currently being processed. The Civil Guard continues with the acquisition of vests, with the purchase forecast of 8809 for this year. I mean, we're going to increase the staffing, we're going to improve salary conditions and also equip the State Security Forces and Corpses. Ladies and gentlemen, these budgets also include investments in information technology and border control. I would like to highlight in this brief intervention the second phase of the development of intelligent borders which will be launched and which will be very important in Ceuta and Melilla, and which will also take place in different ports of the peninsula. I am fully aware of the concern generated by the detention centres for foreigners. As we were not in line with the foreign centres we had, this year a new model of infrastructure has been presented for the IECs, as the Government had committed itself. They have been planned exclusively to carry out this mission, including all the needs to ensure that everyone there is fully guaranteed all his or her rights. The first will be that of Algeciras, a new CIE with the new structure we have planned. Therefore, in order to meet these needs that are generated in the detention centres, we maintain the subsidy for the Spanish Red Cross for assistance programmes in the CIEs. Already last year it increased markedly, from EUR 1 125 000. That is the amount we believe should be established. Let us not forget that in 2015 the amount was 400 000 euros, that in 2016 it was 823,000 euros and that last year and this year it is 1 125 000 euros. Credits for international cooperation aimed at strengthening aid to countries on the African continent to prevent irregular immigration and the fight against criminal organizations that profit from trafficking in human beings - as we have been doing and have been doing by the various governments of Spain - are increasing. I would also like to stress that there is a significant increase of EUR 3 million in a heading which in 2017 was EUR 150 000 to strengthen aid in material resources to the countries of the African continent, thereby reinforcing the prevention of irregular immigration at source. We increase our commitment to the International Organization for Migration to promote the voluntary return of immigrants from third countries. We also have planned investments, as it could not otherwise be, in new police stations and Civil Guard barracks. I am not going to detail it now because it is in the investment annex to which your ladies and gentlemen have had access, but new police and barracks facilities are needed, which will begin to be met with the 2018 budgets, if approved. I do not want to say that these budgets will allow us to maintain the ROCA teams, which are deployed in the rural world, which are working so well and which we increase year after year. Or something that is fundamental to us: the defense of minors. The defence of minors in educational establishments, the fight against school harassment or their training as citizens has become a fundamental pillar of the activities of the State Security Forces and Bodies, within the framework of the management plan of the State Security Secretariat. We will continue to collaborate because it is a commitment that we renew every day, with talks in schools, with meetings with parents. Above all, we must warn of the dangers of using new technologies, which must be used correctly so that minors do not run any risk. I would also like to highlight the work being done by the State Security Forces and Bodies in combating gender-based violence. Within the State Pact against Gender Violence, the Ministry of the Interior, as it could not otherwise be, will also have a prominent role and we are going to make an important budgetary effort. We will increase the services used by police and civilian guards to combat gender-based violence. We will continue to make progress, because it seems to us essential, in the coordination of two systems: the comprehensive monitoring system in cases of gender-based violence, the VioGen system, and the administrative records system of support of the Administration of Justice, the Siraj. They must be coordinated between the two - one managed by the Ministry of the Interior and the other by the Ministry of Justice - and coordinated in turn with the system of registration of penal institutions in order to close the circle, give greater and better protection to women and prevent them from being subjected to gender-based violence. We're going to proceed with the adjustment of the spaces in the police facilities. For what purpose? Very simple: avoid the possible secondary victimization of people who suffer gender-based violence. All police stations and barracks that will be projected from now on and all those that will be subject to renovation and reform will have a reserved space for victims of this type of crime and, in collaboration with the Ministry of Health, Social Services and Equality, a communication campaign will be launched to disseminate and publicize the AlertCops application in version 4.0 among the population, with a special and specific development for victims of gender-based violence who have already filed complaints, especially so that they can learn to use it and can communicate without leaving any trace with the Police or the Civil Guard in case they are in an emergency. Unfortunately, from the Ministry of the Interior we cannot act in most cases until there is no complaint or until it is too late; hence the work that has been carried out since VioGen is very important. That is why the budgets that I am presenting here today and which I hope will be approved will allow us to continue with the accession of the municipalities and the parliaments to this VioGen system in this strategy to work in a coordinated way, thus reaching out to more people, to be more effective and to be, without a doubt, much more positive in addressing this issue. Ladies and gentlemen, another issue of great concern to this ministry is how to deal with emergencies. I would like to highlight the increase in the Emergency Prevention Fund which was created last year with EUR 80 000 and which this year will have a budget of EUR 180 000 - that is, EUR 100 000 more - and which is dedicated, as you know, to preventive activities such as the carrying out of risk analysis, vulnerability and risk analysis, the drawing up of risk maps for civil protection and the implementation of programmes for awareness-raising and preventive information to citizens and education for prevention in schools. Ladies and gentlemen, as you know, this ministry deals with a wide range of issues. One of the issues on which your ladies and gentlemen are usually concerned and which the Ministry and the Government attach great importance and importance to is that of asylum, refuge, statelessness and care for the displaced, to which I assure you that this Government has an important commitment. With this budget we will be able to increase the number of staff performing functions related to the processing of international protection files. They're going to allow me to stop especially at something that, given the circumstances we're going through, I want to highlight in a special way. I want to assure the victims of terrorism that they will continue to be a priority for the Ministry of the Interior and this Government. [[Applauds]] In the face of the announcement of the dissolution of the terrorist band ETA, now, as always, the victims are and will be the first. We will therefore maintain grants to associations, foundations and entities representing and defending the victims of terrorism for EUR 930,000. Also the grants for the two foundations attached to the Ministry of the Interior, the Foundation Centre for the Memory of Victims of Terrorism, which will be 1 650 000 euros, which in large part goes to the rehabilitation and completion works of which it will be its main headquarters, and to the Foundation Victims of Terrorism, with 335 000 euros. Indeed, it is planned that in this exercise the rehabilitation works of the former branch of the Banco de España in Vitoria, which, as your lords know, will be the Memorial Center Victims of Terrorism, and of course we will continue with the compensations derived from Law 29/2011, including a provision for compensations, grants and subsidies to victims of terrorism for the amount of 13.5 million euros. Finally, ladies and gentlemen, I wanted to talk to you about the budget of the Directorate-General for Traffic, which is absolutely linked to our great commitment, which is nothing other than the reduction of victims of traffic accidents and the reduction of the number of accidents that occur; that is the fundamental objective of the ministry in this section. That is why the most important item is that for road safety, with an amount of EUR 766.5 million, and that is why we are working with your ladies and gentlemen on the new road safety strategy. The Ministry has already launched the Plan of Urgent Measures to Reduce Road Accidents, and with this budget we will be able to continue to provide sufficient resources to implement those measures. Before concluding, I would also like to stress the importance and importance of the provision for prison institutions in our budget. Ladies and gentlemen, the repair, maintenance and maintenance of our prisons is permanent and continuous. As we committed ourselves last year, the new centres of Ceuta and Malaga II have been opened, and we do not stay there, we continue to address the modernization of the machinery, the equipment of workshops of own production and the training classes. It is good that you know that we continue to increase the number of staff while the number of prisoners is reduced. Look, since 2009, we have 14 000 fewer inmates in our detention centres. Ladies and gentlemen, we have put forward a serious, rigorous and effective budget, which responds to the main demands of the citizens and contains substantial improvements compared to the previous year. In short, ladies and gentlemen, the fight against crime, terrorism and corruption requires resources and we will not spare them, and above all we will do so to the extent of our possibilities. The assistance and protection of the weakest is another of our objectives and is fully guaranteed, but I would also like to acknowledge the work, effort, dedication and sacrifice of all the officials, of all the bodies that carry out their work in the Ministry of the Interior.
